# Grading bench batch-12

One cached world (Brookside, Delaware, seed fa3bbb297a4b3ea7c25595f71a3f37a7f7fdf08dfd6801c411add18d4f9f0a45), 84 scenes the director staged from records, composed once per profile (warm, cold, stubborn, anxious, boastful) by the same functions play calls. 180 items. Grade each line 1 to 5 on naturalness, prose, sentence structure, length (too short, right, too long), creativity, trait fit and, for lies, plausibility.
